*Chingkwun Lam,Jiaxin Li,Lingfei Zhang,Kuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了稳定性和安全治理内存(SSGM)框架，用于解决自主LLM智能体中动态内存系统面临的记忆腐败、语义漂移和隐私泄露等新兴风险。该框架通过解耦内存演化与执行过程，在内存整合前强制执行一致性验证、时间衰减建模和动态访问控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着记忆系统从静态检索数据库向动态、智能机制转变，出现了记忆治理、语义漂移和隐私漏洞等关键问题。现有的调研主要关注记忆检索效率，忽视了高度动态环境中记忆腐败的新兴风险。

Method: 提出SSGM框架，通过架构分解和形式分析，实现三个核心机制：一致性验证、时间衰减建模和动态访问控制，将内存演化与执行解耦。

Result: SSGM框架可以缓解拓扑诱导的知识泄露(敏感上下文被固化到长期存储)，并防止知识在迭代摘要过程中降解导致的语义漂移。

Conclusion: 本文建立了记忆腐败风险的全面分类法，并为部署安全、持久和可靠的智能记忆系统建立了稳健的治理范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory has emerged as a foundational component of autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents, enabling continuous adaptation, lifelong multimodal learning, and sophisticated reasoning. However, as memory systems transition from static retrieval databases to dynamic, agentic mechanisms, critical concerns regarding memory governance, semantic drift, and privacy vulnerabilities have surfaced. While recent surveys have focused extensively on memory retrieval efficiency, they largely overlook the emergent risks of memory corruption in highly dynamic environments. To address these emerging challenges, we propose the Stability and Safety-Governed Memory (SSGM) framework, a conceptual governance architecture. SSGM decouples memory evolution from execution by enforcing consistency verification, temporal decay modeling, and dynamic access control prior to any memory consolidation. Through formal analysis and architectural decomposition, we show how SSGM can mitigate topology-induced knowledge leakage where sensitive contexts are solidified into long-term storage, and help prevent semantic drift where knowledge degrades through iterative summarization. Ultimately, this work provides a comprehensive taxonomy of memory corruption risks and establishes a robust governance paradigm for deploying safe, persistent, and reliable agentic memory systems.

</details>


### [37] [A Semi-Decentralized Approach to Multiagent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11802)
*Mahdi Al-Husseini,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Kyle H. Wray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SDec-POMDP框架和RS-SDA*算法，用于在通信不确定性环境下实现协作智能体的半去中心化控制，该框架统一了去中心化和多智能体POMDP及现有显式通信机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以有效处理通信不确定性下的多智能体协作控制问题，需要一个能对智能体动作和观察的存储时间进行概率建模的统一框架

Method: 将半去中心化扩展到部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），形成SDec-POMDP框架；提出递归小步半去中心化A*（RS-SDA*）精确算法生成最优策略

Result: RS-SDA*在标准基准测试和海上医疗疏散场景中得到验证，可生成最优策略

Conclusion: 提出了RS-SDA*算法（递归小步半去中心化A*），这是生成最优SDec-POMDP策略的精确算法，在标准基准测试和海上医疗疏散场景中进行了评估。该研究为通过半去中心化视角探索多智能体通信问题提供了良好的理论基础，统一了去中心化和多智能体POMDP以及几种现有的显式通信机制。

Abstract: We introduce an expressive framework and algorithms for the semi-decentralized control of cooperative agents in environments with communication uncertainty. Whereas semi-Markov control admits a distribution over time for agent actions, semi-Markov communication, or what we refer to as semi-decentralization, gives a distribution over time for what actions and observations agents can store in their histories. We extend semi-decentralization to the partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP). The resulting SDec-POMDP unifies decentralized and multiagent POMDPs and several existing explicit communication mechanisms. We present recursive small-step semi-decentralized A* (RS-SDA*), an exact algorithm for generating optimal SDec-POMDP policies. RS-SDA* is evaluated on semi-decentralized versions of several standard benchmarks and a maritime medical evacuation scenario. This paper provides a well-defined theoretical foundation for exploring many classes of multiagent communication problems through the lens of semi-decentralization.

</details>


### [38] [Automating Skill Acquisition through Large-Scale Mining of Open-Source Agentic Repositories: A Framework for Multi-Agent Procedural Knowledge Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11808)
*Shuzhen Bi,Mengsong Wu,Hao Hao,Keqian Li,Wentao Liu,Siyu Song,Hongbo Zhao,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从GitHub等开源仓库自动获取高质量智能体技能的系统框架，通过挖掘TheoremExplainAgent和Code2Video等项目提取Manim可视化与教育能力，实现无需重训练即可扩展LLM程序性知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型虽具备广泛的陈述性知识，但在自主工作流中的程序性专业技能不足，限制了其在实际任务中的效用，因此需要系统性地获取高质量智能体技能来弥补这一差距。

Method: 框架包含三个核心环节：仓库结构分析、基于密集检索的语义技能识别、以及向标准化SKILL.md格式的转换，并结合安全治理与多维评估指标进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，从智能体仓库系统性提取的技能可使教育内容知识传递效率提升40%，且教学品质与人工教程相当。

Conclusion: 通过智能体仓库的系统性提取结合严格的安全治理和多维评估，可实现可扩展的程序性知识获取，有效增强LLM能力而无需模型重训练。

Abstract: The transition from monolithic large language models (LLMs) to modular, skill-equipped agents represents a fundamental architectural shift in artificial intelligence deployment. While general-purpose models demonstrate remarkable breadth in declarative knowledge, their utility in autonomous workflows is frequently constrained by insufficient specialized procedural expertise. This report investigates a systematic framework for automated acquisition of high-quality agent skills through mining of open-source repositories on platforms such as GitHub. We focus on the extraction of visualization and educational capabilities from state-of-the-art systems including TheoremExplainAgent and Code2Video, both utilizing the Manim mathematical animation engine. The framework encompasses repository structural analysis, semantic skill identification through dense retrieval, and translation to the standardized SKILL.md format. We demonstrate that systematic extraction from agentic repositories, combined with rigorous security governance and multi-dimensional evaluation metrics, enables scalable acquisition of procedural knowledge that augments LLM capabilities without requiring model retraining. Our analysis reveals that agent-generated educational content can achieve 40\% gains in knowledge transfer efficiency while maintaining pedagogical quality comparable to human-crafted tutorials.

</details>


### [39] [VisiFold: Long-Term Traffic Forecasting via Temporal Folding Graph and Node Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11816)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Xinyi Du,Weihao Wang,Xuanchi Guo,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisiFold是一种用于长时交通预测的新框架，通过时间折叠图将多时间步快照整合为单图，并采用节点可见性机制（掩码+子图采样）解决计算瓶颈。实验表明该方法在降低资源消耗的同时，即使80%掩码比下仍优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽在短期预测取得进展，但长期预测仍面临挑战。延长预测范围加剧了两大问题：计算资源消耗增加和时空依赖关系更复杂。当前方法存在快照堆叠膨胀和跨步碎片化问题。

Method: 提出时间折叠图框架，将时间快照序列整合为单张图；引入节点可见性机制，结合节点级掩码和子图采样克服大节点数量的计算瓶颈。

Result: VisiFold大幅降低资源消耗的同时，在长时预测任务上优于现有基线。即使在80%的高掩码比下，仍保持性能优势。

Conclusion: VisiFold通过引入时间折叠图和节点可见性机制，有效突破了长时预测中的资源约束，为更现实的长时交通预测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a cornerstone of intelligent transportation systems. While existing research has made significant progress in short-term prediction, long-term forecasting remains a largely uncharted and challenging frontier. Extending the prediction horizon intensifies two critical issues: escalating computational resource consumption and increasingly complex spatial-temporal dependencies. Current approaches, which rely on spatial-temporal graphs and process temporal and spatial dimensions separately, suffer from snapshot-stacking inflation and cross-step fragmentation. To overcome these limitations, we propose \textit{VisiFold}. Our framework introduces a novel temporal folding graph that consolidates a sequence of temporal snapshots into a single graph. Furthermore, we present a node visibility mechanism that incorporates node-level masking and subgraph sampling to overcome the computational bottleneck imposed by large node counts. Extensive experiments show that VisiFold not only drastically reduces resource consumption but also outperforms existing baselines in long-term forecasting tasks. Remarkably, even with a high mask ratio of 80\%, VisiFold maintains its performance advantage. By effectively breaking the resource constraints in both temporal and spatial dimensions, our work paves the way for more realistic long-term traffic forecasting. The code is available at~ https://github.com/PlanckChang/VisiFold.

</details>


### [40] [CreativeBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Machine Creativity via Self-Evolving Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11863)
*Zi-Han Wang,Lam Nguyen,Zhengyang Zhao,Mengyue Yang,Chengwei Qin,Yujiu Yang,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativeBench是一个基于经典认知框架的代码生成机器创造性评估基准，通过统一指标（质量×新颖性）结合逆向工程和自我_play自动化pipeline，客观区分创造性与幻觉。基准包含评估组合创造性的CreativeBench-Combo和评估探索创造性的CreativeBench-Explore两个子集。研究发现规模扩展提升组合创造性但对探索边际收益递减，更大模型变得更正确但更少样，推理能力主要有益于受约束的探索。提出EvoRePE作为即插即用的推理时引导策略来增强机器创造性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量预训练数据的饱和导致研究转向能够持续生成新artifact的进化系统（如AlphaEvolve的成功），但此类系统的进展受到缺乏严格定量评估方法的阻碍。现有评估缺乏客观、可量化的指标来区分真正的创造性与幻觉，因此需要建立系统性的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 提出CreativeBench基准，包含两个子集：CreativeBench-Combo评估组合创造性（通过逆向工程识别组合能力），CreativeBench-Explore评估探索创造性（通过自我_play机制）。采用统一指标（质量×新颖性）来客观区分创造性与幻觉。使用自动化pipeline驱动reverse engineering和self-play进行评估。提出EvoRePE作为即插即用的推理时引导策略，将进化搜索模式内化以提升创造力。

实验方法：评估多个最先进模型，分析规模扩展和推理能力对创造性的影响，探索"扩展收敛"现象，并验证EvoRePE的引导效果。

局限性：主要聚焦代码生成领域；自我_play和逆向工程方法可能引入偏差；EvoRePE的长期效果和跨领域迁移能力待验证。

Result: 研究发现：（1）规模扩展显著提升组合创造性，但对探索任务边际收益递减；（2）更大模型呈现"收敛-by-扩展"现象——变得更正确但更少样（divergent）；（3）推理能力主要有利于受约束的探索而非组合任务。EvoRePE能够持续提升机器创造力。

实验覆盖多个最先进模型，揭示了它们在创造性方面的行为差异。

Conclusion: CreativeBench通过结合质量与新颖性的统一指标，能够客观区分创造性与幻觉，为机器创造性评估提供了严格的基准。EvoRePE作为一种即插即用的推理时引导策略，可持续提升机器创造力。

研究揭示了当前模型的关键局限性：扩展虽能提升组合创造力但对探索的边际收益递减；更大模型呈现"扩展收敛"现象（更正确但更少样）；推理能力主要受限于受约束的探索而非组合任务。

该工作为机器创造性研究奠定了方法论基础，但仍存在局限性：基准测试主要聚焦代码生成领域，其结论在更广泛创造性任务中的泛化性有待验证；自我_play和逆向工程方法可能引入特定偏差；EvoRePE的长期效果和跨领域迁移能力需要进一步评估。

未来可探索将CreativeBench扩展至更广泛的创造性领域（如艺术创作、科学假设生成），并研究更高效的进化策略来突破规模扩展的边界效应。

Abstract: The saturation of high-quality pre-training data has shifted research focus toward evolutionary systems capable of continuously generating novel artifacts, leading to the success of AlphaEvolve. However, the progress of such systems is hindered by the lack of rigorous, quantitative evaluation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce CreativeBench, a benchmark for evaluating machine creativity in code generation, grounded in a classical cognitive framework. Comprising two subsets -- CreativeBench-Combo and CreativeBench-Explore -- the benchmark targets combinatorial and exploratory creativity through an automated pipeline utilizing reverse engineering and self-play. By leveraging executable code, CreativeBench objectively distinguishes creativity from hallucination via a unified metric defined as the product of quality and novelty. Our analysis of state-of-the-art models reveals distinct behaviors: (1) scaling significantly improves combinatorial creativity but yields diminishing returns for exploration; (2) larger models exhibit ``convergence-by-scaling,'' becoming more correct but less divergent; and (3) reasoning capabilities primarily benefit constrained exploration rather than combination. Finally, we propose EvoRePE, a plug-and-play inference-time steering strategy that internalizes evolutionary search patterns to consistently enhance machine creativity.

</details>


### [41] [AdaFuse: Accelerating Dynamic Adapter Inference via Token-Level Pre-Gating and Fused Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11873)
*Qiyang Li,Rui Kong,Yuchen Li,Hengyi Cai,Shuaiqiang Wang,Linghe Kong,Guihai Chen,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaFuse通过token级别预门控策略和融合切换内核，实现MoE与参数高效适配器（如LoRA）的高效结合，在保持精度的同时将推理延迟降低超过2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏结构（如MoE）与参数高效适配器（LoRA）的集成虽然能增强LLM性能，但导致推理延迟急剧上升（降低超过2.5倍），主要瓶颈在于传统动态路由带来的碎片化、顺序CUDA内核启动开销。

Method: 提出token级别预门控策略，在token处理前做出全局路由决策（"一次决策，处处应用"），并将所有选定LoRA适配器参数融合到主干模型中，通过自定义CUDA内核执行融合切换操作。

Result: 在开源LLM上的实验表明，AdaFuse达到与先进动态适配器相当的精度，同时将解码延迟降低超过2.4倍。

Conclusion: AdaFuse通过算法与底层硬件的协同设计，有效弥合了模型能力与推理效率之间的差距，为高效动态适配器执行提供了新范式。

Abstract: The integration of dynamic, sparse structures like Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) with parameter-efficient adapters (e.g., LoRA) is a powerful technique for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs). However, this architectural enhancement comes at a steep cost: despite minimal increases in computational load, the inference latency often skyrockets, leading to decoding speeds slowing by over 2.5 times. Through a fine-grained performance analysis, we pinpoint the primary bottleneck not in the computation itself, but in the severe overhead from fragmented, sequential CUDA kernel launches required for conventional dynamic routing. To address this challenge, we introduce AdaFuse, a framework built on a tight co-design between the algorithm and the underlying hardware system to enable efficient dynamic adapter execution. Departing from conventional layer-wise or block-wise routing, AdaFuse employs a token-level pre-gating strategy, which makes a single, global routing decision for all adapter layers before a token is processed. This "decide-once, apply-everywhere" approach effectively staticizes the execution path for each token, creating an opportunity for holistic optimization. We capitalize on this by developing a custom CUDA kernel that performs a fused switching operation, merging the parameters of all selected LoRA adapters into the backbone model in a single, efficient pass. Experimental results on popular open-source LLMs show that AdaFuse achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art dynamic adapters while drastically cutting decoding latency by a factor of over 2.4x, thereby bridging the gap between model capability and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [42] [Fair Learning for Bias Mitigation and Quality Optimization in Paper Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11936)
*Uttamasha Anjally Oyshi,Susan Gauch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fair-PaperRec是一个基于多层感知器(MLP)的模型,旨在解决论文审稿中 underrepresented groups 面临的人口统计学偏见,同时保持高质量要求。该方法通过交叉标准(如种族、国家)和定制公平性损失来惩罚人口统计学差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管采用双盲审稿,作者的人口统计学偏见仍然使 underrepresented groups 处于劣势地位,需要一种方法来在保持学术质量的同时促进公平性。

Method: 使用MLP模型,结合交叉标准(种族、国家)和定制公平性损失函数,在惩罚人口统计学差异的同时保留质量要求。

Result: 在ACM SIGCHI、DIS和IUI会议数据上的评估表明,underrepresented group 参与度提高42.03%,整体效用提升3.16%。

Conclusion: 多样性促进不会损害学术严谨性,支持以公平为重点的同行评审解决方案,实现了 equity 和 quality 的平衡。

Abstract: Despite frequent double-blind review, demographic biases of authors still disadvantage the underrepresented groups. We present Fair-PaperRec, a MultiLayer Perceptron (MLP)-based model that addresses demographic disparities in post-review paper acceptance decisions while maintaining high-quality requirements. Our methodology penalizes demographic disparities while preserving quality through intersectional criteria (e.g., race, country) and a customized fairness loss, in contrast to heuristic approaches. Evaluations using conference data from ACM Special Interest Group on Computer-Human Interaction (SIGCHI), Designing Interactive Systems (DIS), and Intelligent User Interfaces (IUI) indicate a 42.03% increase in underrepresented group participation and a 3.16% improvement in overall utility, indicating that diversity promotion does not compromise academic rigor and supports equity-focused peer review solutions.

</details>


### [43] [Prototype-Based Knowledge Guidance for Fine-Grained Structured Radiology Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11938)
*Chantal Pellegrini,Adrian Delchev,Ege Özsoy,Nassir Navab,Matthias Keicher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProtoSR方法，通过从大规模自由文本报告中挖掘信息来增强结构化放射学报告的生成。利用指令微调LLM从MIMIC-CXR中提取80k+研究构建多模态知识库，使用视觉原型表示答案选项，并通过原型检索和残差连接来增强模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化放射学报告比自由文本更具优势，但自动化困难，因为模型需要从有限的结构化监督中做出关于罕见发现和属性的细粒度离散决策。自由文本报告在常规护理中大规模产生，通过详细描述隐含编码细粒度图像关联信息，但这些信息未被充分利用。

Method: 1. 使用指令微调LLM构建自动提取管道，从80k+ MIMIC-CXR研究中挖掘信息；2. 构建与结构化报告模板对齐的多模态知识库，用视觉原型表示每个答案选项；3. 训练ProtoSR检索与当前图像-问题对相关的原型；4. 通过原型条件残差增强模型预测，提供数据驱动的第二意见选择性纠正预测。

Result: 在Rad-ReStruct基准测试中达到最先进水平，在详细属性问题上有最大改进，证明了整合自由文本衍生信号对细粒度图像理解的价值。

Conclusion: ProtoSR成功展示了如何利用自由文本报告中隐含的丰富信息来增强结构化报告生成，为放射学报告自动化提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: Structured radiology reporting promises faster, more consistent communication than free text, but automation remains difficult as models must make many fine-grained, discrete decisions about rare findings and attributes from limited structured supervision. In contrast, free-text reports are produced at scale in routine care and implicitly encode fine-grained, image-linked information through detailed descriptions. To leverage this unstructured knowledge, we propose ProtoSR, an approach for injecting free-text information into structured report population. First, we introduce an automatic extraction pipeline that uses an instruction-tuned LLM to mine 80k+ MIMIC-CXR studies and build a multimodal knowledge base aligned with a structured reporting template, representing each answer option with a visual prototype. Using this knowledge base, ProtoSR is trained to retrieve prototypes relevant for the current image-question pair and augment the model predictions through a prototype-conditioned residual, providing a data-driven second opinion that selectively corrects predictions. On the Rad-ReStruct benchmark, ProtoSR achieves state-of-the-art results, with the largest improvements on detailed attribute questions, demonstrating the value of integrating free-text derived signal for fine-grained image understanding.

</details>


### [44] [Learning Transferable Sensor Models via Language-Informed Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11950)
*Yuliang Chen,Arvind Pillai,Yu Yvonne Wu,Tess Z. Griffin,Lisa Marsch,Michael V. Heinz,Nicholas C. Jacobson,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SLIP是一个开源框架，通过对比对齐和传感器条件标题生成学习语言对齐表示，支持可变时间分辨率和变长输入，无需额外重新训练即可泛化到不同传感器配置。在11个数据集上实现了77.14%的线性探测准确率和64.83%的问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代传感系统生成大量未标记的多变量时间序列数据。现有的自监督学习(SSL)方法主要针对重建或预测目标进行优化，往往无法捕获下游分类和推理任务所需的语义结构。尽管最近的传感器-语言对齐方法通过标题生成和零样本迁移提高了语义泛化能力，但它们仅限于固定传感器配置（如预定义的通道集、信号长度或时间分辨率），阻碍了跨域应用。

Method: 提出SLIP(Sensor Language-Informed Pretuning)框架，通过以下方式学习跨不同传感器设置泛化的语言对齐表示：(1)将对比对齐与传感器条件标题生成相结合，促进判别性理解和生成性推理；(2)重新利用预训练的解码器语言模型，通过交叉注意力机制；(3)引入灵活的可变长度的patch嵌入器，支持不同时间分辨率和可变长度输入，推理时无需额外重新训练。

Result: 在11个数据集上，SLIP在零样本迁移、信号标题生成和问答任务上展现出卓越性能。线性探测平均准确率达到77.14%，比强基线方法相对提升5.93%，传感器问答准确率达到64.83%。

Conclusion: SLIP成功实现了跨不同传感器配置的语义泛化，统一了判别理解和生成推理能力，为多变量时间序列的自监督学习提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Modern sensing systems generate large volumes of unlabeled multivariate time-series data. This abundance of unlabeled data makes self-supervised learning (SSL) a natural approach for learning transferable representations. However, most existing approaches are optimized for reconstruction or forecasting objectives and often fail to capture the semantic structure required for downstream classification and reasoning tasks. While recent sensor-language alignment methods improve semantic generalization through captioning and zero-shot transfer, they are limited to fixed sensor configurations, such as predefined channel sets, signal lengths, or temporal resolutions, which hinders cross-domain applicability. To address these gaps, we introduce \textbf{SLIP} (\textbf{S}ensor \textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{P}retraining), an open-source framework for learning language-aligned representations that generalize across diverse sensor setups. SLIP integrates contrastive alignment with sensor-conditioned captioning, facilitating both discriminative understanding and generative reasoning. By repurposing a pretrained decoder-only language model via cross-attention and introducing an elegant, flexible patch-embedder, SLIP supports different temporal resolutions and variable-length input at inference time without additional retraining. Across 11 datasets, SLIP demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot transfer, signal captioning, and question answering. It achieves a 77.14% average linear-probing accuracy, a 5.93% relative improvement over strong baselines, and reaches 64.83% accuracy in sensor-based question answering.

</details>


### [45] [Normative Common Ground Replication (NormCoRe): Replication-by-Translation for Studying Norms in Multi-agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11974)
*Luca Deck,Simeon Allmendinger,Lucas Müller,Niklas Kühl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormCoRe框架，将人类被试实验设计系统性地转化为多智能体人工智能(MAAI)环境，并通过复制"无知之幕"下的分配正义实验来演示其效用。研究发现AI智能体的规范性判断与人类基准存在差异，且对基础模型选择和智能体角色语言敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将规范视为对齐或复制的目标，隐含假设人类被试与AI智能体等价，导致集体规范性动态研究不足。需要一个系统性的方法将人类实验设计转化为MAAI环境。

Method: 提出NormCoRe（规范性共识复制）方法论框架，将人类被试研究的结构层映射到AI智能体研究设计。复制了"无知之幕"下的分配正义经典实验。

Result: AI智能体研究中的规范性判断可能与人类基准不同，且对基础模型选择和用于实例化智能体角色的人类语言敏感。

Conclusion: NormCoRe为MAAI中的规范分析提供了原则性途径，有助于指导、反思和记录使用AI智能体自动化或支持原本由人类执行任务时的设计选择。

Abstract: In the late 2010s, the fashion trend NormCore framed sameness as a signal of belonging, illustrating how norms emerge through collective coordination. Today, similar forms of normative coordination can be observed in systems based on Multi-agent Artificial Intelligence (MAAI), as AI-based agents deliberate, negotiate, and converge on shared decisions in fairness-sensitive domains. Yet, existing empirical approaches often treat norms as targets for alignment or replication, implicitly assuming equivalence between human subjects and AI agents and leaving collective normative dynamics insufficiently examined. To address this gap, we propose Normative Common Ground Replication (NormCoRe), a novel methodological framework to systematically translate the design of human subject experiments into MAAI environments. Building on behavioral science, replication research, and state-of-the-art MAAI architectures, NormCoRe maps the structural layers of human subject studies onto the design of AI agent studies, enabling systematic documentation of study design and analysis of norms in MAAI. We demonstrate the utility of NormCoRe by replicating a seminal experimental study on distributive justice, in which participants negotiate fairness principles under a "veil of ignorance". We show that normative judgments in AI agent studies can differ from human baselines and are sensitive to the choice of the foundation model and the language used to instantiate agent personas. Our work provides a principled pathway for analyzing norms in MAAI and helps to guide, reflect, and document design choices whenever AI agents are used to automate or support tasks formerly carried out by humans.

</details>


### [46] [LABSHIELD: A Multimodal Benchmark for Safety-Critical Reasoning and Planning in Scientific Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11987)
*Qianpu Sun,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Ying Li,Kuangzhi Ge,Jiajun Li,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LABSHIELD是首个基于OSHA和GHS标准的实验室安全多视角基准测试，评估32个MLLM在危险识别和安全推理方面的能力，结果显示模型在专业实验室场景中平均下降32%，凸显安全中心推理框架的迫切需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current MLLM agents evolving from lab assistants to self-driving lab operators require rigorous safety evaluation, as existing safety awareness and decision-making reliability in high-stakes laboratory settings remain insufficiently defined.

Method: Dual-track evaluation framework assessing 32 models (20 proprietary, 9 open-source, 3 embodied) on 164 operational tasks grounded in OSHA standards and GHS taxonomy.

Result: Models show 32.0% average performance drop from general-domain MCQ accuracy to Semi-open QA safety performance, particularly in hazard interpretation and safety-aware planning.

Conclusion: LABSHIELD benchmark reveals significant safety reasoning gaps in MLLMs, with 32% performance drop in professional laboratory scenarios, highlighting urgent need for safety-centric reasoning frameworks for autonomous scientific experimentation.

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly catalyzing scientific automation, with multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents evolving from lab assistants into self-driving lab operators. This transition imposes stringent safety requirements on laboratory environments, where fragile glassware, hazardous substances, and high-precision laboratory equipment render planning errors or misinterpreted risks potentially irreversible. However, the safety awareness and decision-making reliability of embodied agents in such high-stakes settings remain insufficiently defined and evaluated. To bridge this gap, we introduce LABSHIELD, a realistic multi-view benchmark designed to assess MLLMs in hazard identification and safety-critical reasoning. Grounded in U.S. Occupational Safety and Health Administration (OSHA) standards and the Globally Harmonized System (GHS), LABSHIELD establishes a rigorous safety taxonomy spanning 164 operational tasks with diverse manipulation complexities and risk profiles. We evaluate 20 proprietary models, 9 open-source models, and 3 embodied models under a dual-track evaluation framework. Our results reveal a systematic gap between general-domain MCQ accuracy and Semi-open QA safety performance, with models exhibiting an average drop of 32.0% in professional laboratory scenarios, particularly in hazard interpretation and safety-aware planning. These findings underscore the urgent necessity for safety-centric reasoning frameworks to ensure reliable autonomous scientific experimentation in embodied laboratory contexts. The full dataset will be released soon.

</details>


### [47] [Few-for-Many Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11992)
*Ping Guo,Tiantian Zhang,Xi Lin,Xiang Li,Zhi-Ri Tang,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将个性化联邦学习(PFL)重新定义为"少对多"优化问题，仅维护K个共享服务器模型(K<<M)来服务所有M个客户端，提出FedFew算法通过梯度更新联合优化K个服务器模型，实验表明仅用3个模型即可超越最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法依赖聚类或模型插值等启发式方法，缺乏平衡异构客户端目标的 principled 机制；维护M个独立模型在数百或数千客户端场景下存在显著可扩展性挑战。

Method: 将PFL重新表述为few-for-many优化问题，仅维护K个共享服务器模型(K<<M)来服务M个客户端；提出FedFew算法，通过高效梯度更新联合优化K个服务器模型，自动发现最优模型多样性。

Result: 理论证明：近似误差随K增加而减小，每个客户端模型随数据增长收敛到各自最优。实验：在视觉、NLP和真实医学影像数据集上，仅用3个模型 consistently outperforms 其他SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了近最优个性化：近似误差随K增大而减小，客户端模型随数据量增加收敛到各自最优。FedFew无需手动客户端划分或超参数调优，即可通过优化过程自动发现最优模型多样性。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to train customized models for clients with highly heterogeneous data distributions while preserving data privacy. Existing approaches often rely on heuristics like clustering or model interpolation, which lack principled mechanisms for balancing heterogeneous client objectives. Serving $M$ clients with distinct data distributions is inherently a multi-objective optimization problem, where achieving optimal personalization ideally requires $M$ distinct models on the Pareto front. However, maintaining $M$ separate models poses significant scalability challenges in federated settings with hundreds or thousands of clients. To address this challenge, we reformulate PFL as a few-for-many optimization problem that maintains only $K$ shared server models ($K \ll M$) to collectively serve all $M$ clients. We prove that this framework achieves near-optimal personalization: the approximation error diminishes as $K$ increases and each client's model converges to each client's optimum as data grows. Building on this reformulation, we propose FedFew, a practical algorithm that jointly optimizes the $K$ server models through efficient gradient-based updates. Unlike clustering-based approaches that require manual client partitioning or interpolation-based methods that demand careful hyperparameter tuning, FedFew automatically discovers the optimal model diversity through its optimization process. Experiments across vision, NLP, and real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that FedFew, with just 3 models, consistently outperforms other state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/pgg3/FedFew.

</details>


### [48] [Can RL Improve Generalization of LLM Agents? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12011)
*Zhiheng Xi,Xin Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Jiazheng Zhang,Yutao Fan,Zhihao Zhang,Shichun Liu,Mingxu Chai,Xiaowei Shi,Yitao Zhai,Xunliang Cai,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化微调(RFT)训练的LLM智能体在跨环境泛化方面的表现，揭示了其在环境内任务难度泛化良好但跨环境迁移较弱的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFT研究主要在同环境下评估，缺乏对智能体在真实部署场景中面对未见环境（不同背景知识、观察空间和动作接口）时泛化能力的理解。

Method: 从三个维度系统评估RFT的泛化能力：(1)环境内任务难度泛化；(2)跨环境迁移到未见环境；(3)顺序多环境训练以量化迁移和遗忘。通过在不同环境配置下训练和测试LLM智能体进行实验。

Result: RFT在环境内任务难度上泛化良好，但跨环境迁移能力较弱，这与语义先验、观察/动作接口的差异相关。顺序训练可带来下游收益且上游遗忘很小，混合环境训练可改善整体平衡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了RFT泛化能力的边界和影响因素，为开发更具泛化性的LLM智能体提供了实证依据和方向指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) has shown promise for training LLM agents to perform multi-turn decision-making based on environment feedback. However, most existing evaluations remain largely in-domain: training and testing are conducted in the same environment or even on the same tasks. In real-world deployment, agents may operate in unseen environments with different background knowledge, observation spaces, and action interfaces. To characterize the generalization profile of RFT under such shifts, we conduct a systematic study along three axes: (1) within-environment generalization across task difficulty, (2) cross-environment transfer to unseen environments, and (3) sequential multi-environment training to quantify transfer and forgetting. Our results show that RFT generalizes well across task difficulty within an environment, but exhibits weaker transfer to unseen environments, which correlates with shifts in both semantic priors and observation/action interfaces. In contrast, sequential training yields promising downstream gains with minimal upstream forgetting, and mixture training across environments improves the overall balance. We further provide detailed analyses and deeper insights, and hope our work helps the community develop and deploy generalizable LLM agents.

</details>


### [49] [A Robust and Efficient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12096)
*Sheng-You Huang,Hsiao-Chuan Chang,Yen-Chi Chen,Ting-Han Wei,I-Hau Yeh,Sheng-Yao Kuan,Chien-Yao Wang,Hsuan-Han Lee,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper proposes a robust MARL framework for traffic signal control that integrates Turning Ratio Randomization training, Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, and Neighbor-Based Observation with MAPPO/CTDE. The framework achieves over 10% reduction in average waiting time and demonstrates superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reinforcement Learning in Traffic Signal Control faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations.

Method: The paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework with three key mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization as a training strategy to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space for balancing responsiveness and precision; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme using MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE), enabling centralized updates while maintaining scalable local communication.

Result: Experimental results demonstrate that the framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability.

Conclusion: The proposed MARL framework offers a practical solution for adaptive signal control, demonstrating superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintaining high control stability. The framework successfully integrates Turning Ratio Randomization, Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment, and Neighbor-Based Observation to address key challenges in real-world TSC deployment.

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Traffic Signal Control (TSC) faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations. This paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework validated in the Vissim traffic simulator. The framework integrates three mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization, a training strategy that exposes agents to dynamic turning probabilities to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, which balances responsiveness and precision through cyclical, exponential phase adjustments; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme utilizing the MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE). By leveraging centralized updates, this approach approximates the efficacy of global observations while maintaining scalable local communication. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability, offering a practical solution for adaptive signal control.

</details>


### [50] [TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12133)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Nils Hoehing,Janak Kapuriya,Arjun Karuvally,Ellen Rushe,Anthony Ventresque,Noel O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入TopoBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在拓扑网格谜题上的推理能力，发现即使前沿模型在困难实例上表现不佳，且瓶颈主要在于从空间表示中提取约束而非推理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑网格谜题需要全局空间不变量（如连通性、环闭合、区域对称性）的推理，即使是最强大的大语言模型也难以解决。研究在受控环境下评估LLM的这些能力具有重要意义。

Method: 构建了包含六个谜题家族、三个难度等级的TopoBench基准；标注了750条思维链轨迹并建立错误分类体系以识别四种候选失败模式；通过针对性干预模拟各类错误进行测试；研究了三类缓解策略（提示引导、单元格对齐的网格表示、基于工具的约束检查）。

Result: 前沿模型在困难实例上的解决率不足四分之一，其中两个谜题家族几乎无法解决。干预实验表明：过早承诺和约束遗忘直接影响解题能力，而重复推理是搜索的良性副作用。缓解策略验证发现瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束，而非约束推理本身。

Conclusion: LLM在拓扑网格谜题上的失败主要源于从空间表示中提取和维持拓扑约束的困难，而非推理能力的不足。这一发现为未来改进LLM的空间推理能力提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.

</details>


### [51] [Compiling Temporal Numeric Planning into Discrete PDDL+: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12188)
*Andrea Micheli,Enrico Scala,Alessandro Valentini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从PDDL 2.1时间规划到PDDL+的多项式编译方法，该方法完全捕获语义，仅需动作非自重叠假设，保持计划长度至常数因子，实验验证对困难时间数值问题具有实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL+建模语言引入后，学界已知可将带持续动作的时间规划编译为PDDL+，但从未提出过实际可行的编译方案。

Method: 多项式时间编译方法，仅假设动作非自重叠，保持计划长度至常数因子。

Result: 实验表明该编译方法对困难的时间数值问题具有实际相关性。

Conclusion: 提出的编译方法在处理困难时间数值问题上具有实际相关性，验证了从PDDL 2.1到PDDL+的编译可行性。

Abstract: Since the introduction of the PDDL+ modeling language, it was known that temporal planning with durative actions (as in PDDL 2.1) could be compiled into PDDL+. However, no practical compilation was presented in the literature ever since. We present a practical compilation from temporal planning with durative actions into PDDL+, fully capturing the semantics and only assuming the non-self-overlapping of actions. Our compilation is polynomial, retains the plan length up to a constant factor and is experimentally shown to be of practical relevance for hard temporal numeric problems.

</details>


### [52] [Portfolio of Solving Strategies in CEGAR-based Object Packing and Scheduling for Sequential 3D Printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12224)
*Pavel Surynek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ算法，通过并行化CEGAR-SEQ算法来解决顺序3D打印中的物体排列和调度问题。该方法利用现代多核个人计算机CPU的计算能力，通过投资组合策略并行执行多种物体排列方法。实验表明，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ在处理多批次打印板时通常使用比CEGAR-SEQ更少的打印板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去仅在超级计算机中可用的计算能力（尤其是并行计算能力）如今已普及到标准个人电脑和移动电话CPU中。作者旨在有效利用这些现代多核CPU的计算能力来解决3D打印中复杂的物体排列和调度组合问题。此外，原始CEGAR-SEQ算法仅使用将物体放置在打印板中心的策略，存在局限性。

Method: 作者通过以下方法实现目标：1)将顺序物体排列和调度问题表达为线性算术公式；2)采用类似于反例引导抽象细化(CEGAR)的技术进行求解；3)提出替代物体排列策略（如将物体放置在打印板角落、按高度调度）；4)在高层面上并行化CEGAR-SEQ算法，执行具有多种物体排列策略的投资组合，形成Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ算法。

Result: 实验评估表明，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ优于原始CEGAR-SEQ算法。当调度多个打印板的物体批次时，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ通常使用比CEGAR-SEQ更少的打印板。

Conclusion: 通过投资组合策略并行化CEGAR-SEQ算法是有效的，能够显著提升3D打印调度问题的求解性能，充分利用现代多核CPU的并行计算能力。

Abstract: Computing power that used to be available only in supercomputers decades ago especially their parallelism is currently available in standard personal computer CPUs even in CPUs for mobile telephones. We show how to effectively utilize the computing power of modern multi-core personal computer CPU to solve the complex combinatorial problem of object arrangement and scheduling for sequential 3D printing. We achieved this by parallelizing the existing CEGAR-SEQ algorithm that solves the sequential object arrangement and scheduling by expressing it as a linear arithmetic formula which is then solved by a technique inspired by counterexample guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR). The original CEGAR-SEQ algorithm uses an object arrangement strategy that places objects towards the center of the printing plate. We propose alternative object arrangement strategies such as placing objects towards a corner of the printing plate and scheduling objects according to their height. Our parallelization is done at the high-level where we execute the CEGAR-SEQ algorithm in parallel with a portfolio of object arrangement strategies, an algorithm is called Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ. Our experimental evaluation indicates that Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ outperforms the original CEGAR-SEQ. When a batch of objects for multiple printing plates is scheduled, Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ often uses fewer printing plates than CEGAR-SEQ.

</details>

*Yixin Liu,Yue Yu,DiJia Su,Sid Wang,Xuewei Wang,Song Jiang,Bo Liu,Arman Cohan,Yuandong Tian,Zhengxing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges, which can benefit from inference-time scaling, provide a promising path for extending the success of reasoning models to non-verifiable domains where the output correctness/quality cannot be directly checked. However, while reasoning judges have shown better performance on static evaluation benchmarks, their effectiveness in actual policy training has not been systematically examined. Therefore, we conduct a rigorous study to investigate the actual impact of non-reasoning and reasoning judges in reinforcement-learning-based LLM alignment. Our controlled synthetic setting, where a "gold-standard" judge (gpt-oss-120b) provides preference annotations to train smaller judges, reveals key differences between non-reasoning and reasoning judges: non-reasoning judges lead to reward hacking easily, while reasoning judges can lead to policies that achieve strong performance when evaluated by the gold-standard judge. Interestingly, we find that the reasoning-judge-trained policies achieve such strong performance by learning to generate highly effective adversarial outputs that can also score well on popular benchmarks such as Arena-Hard by deceiving other LLM-judges. Combined with our further analysis, our study highlights both important findings and room for improvements for applying (reasoning) LLM-judges in non-verifiable LLM post-training.

</details>

### [54] [BEACON: Budget-Aware Entity Matching Across Domains (Extended Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11391)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Roee Shraga,Gregory Goren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BEACON是一个针对低资源跨领域实体匹配的概率感知、预算感知框架，通过利用成对候选匹配的嵌入表示来指导域外样本的有效选择，在有限的标注数据下训练准确的领域特定实体匹配模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习虽然提升了实体匹配性能，但微调通常需要大量标注数据，成本高昂。在电商匹配场景中，不同领域的标注数据稀缺程度差异很大，如何在有限标注数据的情况下智能训练准确的领域特定EM模型是一个关键挑战。

Method: BEACON框架的核心洞察是：成对候选匹配的嵌入表示可以指导在有限领域内监督下有效选择域外样本。框架具有分布感知和预算感知特性，能够在给定训练预算约束下优化样本选择策略。

Result: 在多个来自EM基准测试的领域划分数据集上进行了广泛实验，证明BEACON在不同训练预算下均一致优于最先进的现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的低资源跨领域实体匹配解决方案，通过利用嵌入表示引导样本选择，能够在少量标注数据条件下构建准确的领域特定EM模型，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM)--the task of determining whether two data records refer to the same real-world entity--is a core task in data integration. Recent advances in deep learning have set a new standard for EM, particularly through fine-tuning Pretrained Language Models (PLMs) and, more recently, Large Language Models (LLMs). However, fine-tuning typically requires large amounts of labeled data, which are expensive and time-consuming to obtain. In the context of e-commerce matching, labeling scarcity varies widely across domains, raising the question of how to intelligently train accurate domain-specific EM models with limited labeled data. In this work we assume users have only limited amount of labels for a specific target domain but have access to labeled data from other domains. We introduce BEACON, a distribution-aware, budget-aware framework for low-resource EM across domains. BEACON leverages the insight that embedding representations of pairwise candidate matches can guide the effective selection of out-of-domain samples under limited in-domain supervision. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple domain-partitioned datasets derived from established EM benchmarks, demonstrating that BEACON consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under different training budgets.

</details>


### [55] [Faster Relational Algorithms Using Geometric Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11402)
*Aryan Esmailpour,Stavros Sintos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RBBD树框架，利用几何数据结构加速关系数据上的聚类等优化任务。通过在关系连接上进行高效采样和计数，实现RBBD树的即时展开，避免物化连接结果。\n


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据上的优化任务（如聚类）需要执行代价高昂的连接操作才能访问完整数据集。虽然BBD树等几何数据结构在标准计算环境中能产生快速近似算法，但其应用于关系数据时面临连接输出规模过大的问题。\n

Method: 开发了针对关系环境定制的随机化BBD树（RBBD树）。通过利用关系连接上的高效采样和计数技术，仅在需要时即时构建RBBD树的相关部分，而非完全物化整个树结构，从而模拟几何查询程序。\n

Result: 作为应用，提出关系数据上k-中心/k-均值/k-中位数聚类算法，在保持相同近似保证的前提下，将运行时间改进k倍。该方法具有通用性，可应用于关系设置中的各种优化问题。\n

Conclusion: 该工作成功将几何方法引入关系数据计算领域，通过在关系连接上采样和计数的技术创新，解决了连接结果物化的高成本问题，为关系数据上的优化问题提供了更高效的算法框架。\n

Abstract: Optimization tasks over relational data, such as clustering, often suffer from the prohibitive cost of join operations, which are necessary to access the full dataset. While geometric data structures like BBD trees yield fast approximation algorithms in the standard computational setting, their application to relational data remains unclear due to the size of the join output. In this paper, we introduce a framework that leverages geometric insights to design faster algorithms when the data is stored as the results of a join query in a relational database. Our core contribution is the development of the RBBD tree, a randomized variant of the BBD tree tailored for relational settings. Instead of completely constructing the RBBD tree, by leveraging efficient sampling and counting techniques over relational joins, we enable on-the-fly efficient expansion of the RBBD tree, maintaining only the necessary parts. This allows us to simulate geometric query procedures without materializing the join result. As an application, we present algorithms that improve the state-of-the-art for relational $k$-center/means/median clustering by a factor of $k$ in running time while maintaining the same approximation guarantees. Our method is general and can be applied to various optimization problems in the relational setting.

</details>


### [56] [LHGstore: An In-Memory Learned Graph Storage for Fast Updates and Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11596)
*Pengpeng Qiao,Zhiwei Zhang,Xinzhou Wang,Zhetao Li,Xiaochun Cao,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LHGstore是首个将学习索引集成到图管理的内存图存储系统，通过设计两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问，并根据度数自适应选择数据结构（低度顶点用轻量数组，高度顶点用学习索引），在保持遍历局部性的同时提升更新吞吐量。实验表明其吞吐量比SOTA系统高5.9-28.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用依赖内存动态图，需同时处理频繁更新和低延迟分析。但更新效率与遍历局部性之间存在权衡，且高度偏斜的度数分布使该问题更加复杂。现有图索引方案难以同时优化这两个目标。

Method: 设计两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问；根据顶点度数自适应选择数据结构——低度顶点使用轻量数组最大化遍历局部性，高度顶点应用学习索引提升更新吞吐量。

Result: LHGstore吞吐量比SOTA内存图存储系统高5.9-28.2倍，analytics性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 首次成功将学习索引应用于图管理领域，通过度数感知的层次化设计有效解决了更新效率与遍历局部性的权衡问题，为内存图存储提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Various real-world applications rely on in-memory dynamic graphs that must efficiently handle frequent updates while supporting low-latency analytics on evolving structures. Achieving both objectives remains challenging due to the trade-off between update efficiency and traversal locality, particularly under highly skewed degree distributions. This motivates the design of graph indexing schemes optimized for in-memory graph management on modern multi-core CPUs. We present LHGstore, a degree-aware Learned Hierarchical Graph storage that, for the first time, integrates learned indexing into graph management. LHGstore designs a two-level hierarchy that decouples vertex and edge access and further organizes each vertex's edges using data structures adaptive to its degree. Lightweight arrays are used for low-degree vertices to maximize traversal locality, while learned indexes are applied to high-degree vertices to improve update throughput. Extensive experiments show that LHGstore achieves 5.9-28.2$\times$ higher throughput and significantly faster analytics than SOTA in-memory graph storage systems.

</details>


### [57] [Sema: A High-performance System for LLM-based Semantic Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11622)
*Kangkang Qi,Dongyang Xie,Wenbo Li,Hao Zhang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Kangfei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Sema是一个基于DuckDB的高性能语义查询引擎，将LLM语义操作符作为一等公民，通过SemaSQL方言在SQL中注入自然语言表达式，结合逻辑优化和自适应查询执行实现token消耗与延迟的Pareto最优平衡，实验显示2-10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据分析系统存在三个问题：DataFrame原语缺乏现代DBMS的高效执行基础设施；SQL UDF将语义逻辑与查询优化器隔离且增加用户实现复杂度；LLM语义操作符的高成本和不确定性使得传统优化规则和成本模型失效。

Method: 提出Sema系统，基于DuckDB构建，包含：(1) SemaSQL声明式方言，支持将自然语言表达式无缝注入标准SQL子句；(2) 逻辑优化器压缩自然语言表达式并从语义操作符推导关系约束；(3) 运行时采用自适应查询执行(AQE)动态重排操作符、融合语义操作和应用提示批处理。

Result: 在20个语义查询任务（分类、摘要、提取）上的实验表明，Sema相比三个基线系统实现2-10倍加速，同时保持竞争力的结果质量。

Conclusion: Sema通过将LLM作为一等公民集成到DBMS中，实现了在准确率约束下平衡token消耗和延迟的Pareto最优执行路径，在语义查询任务上比基线系统快2-10倍。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into data analytics has unlocked powerful capabilities for reasoning over bulk structured and unstructured data. However, existing systems typically rely on either DataFrame primitives, which lack the efficient execution infrastructure of modern DBMSs, or SQL User-Defined Functions (UDFs), which isolate semantic logic from the query optimizer and burden users with implementation complexities. The LLM-powered semantic operators also bring new challenges due to the high cost and non-deterministic nature of LLM invocation, where conventional optimization rules and cost models are inapplicable for their optimization.
  To bridge these gaps, we present Sema, a high-performance semantic query engine built on DuckDB that treats LLM-powered semantic operators as first-class citizens. Sema introduces SemaSQL, a declarative dialect that allows users seamlessly inject natural language expressions into standard SQL clauses, enabling end-to-end optimization and execution. At the logical level, the optimizer of Sema compresses natural language expressions and deduces relational constraints from semantic operators. At runtime, Sema employs Adaptive Query Execution (AQE) to dynamically reorder operators, fuse semantic operations, and apply prompt batching. This approach seeks a Pareto-optimal execution path balancing token consumption and latency under accuracy constraints. We evaluate Sema on 20 semantic queries across classification, summarization, and extraction tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that Sema achieves $2-10 \times$ speedup against three baseline systems while achieving competitive result quality.

</details>


### [58] [OMNIA: Closing the Loop by Leveraging LLMs for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11820)
*Frédéric Ieng,Soror Sahri,Mourad Ouzzani,Massinissa Hammaz,Salima Benbernou,Hanieh Khorashadizadeh,Sven Groppe,Farah Benamara*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMNIA是一种两阶段知识图谱补全方法，结合聚类、嵌入过滤和LLM语义验证来推断缺失三元组，兼顾图结构语义和语言模型语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识图谱自动构建尤其是使用LLM时容易产生不完整或噪声数据；现有方法存在局限性：结构嵌入忽略语义，而语言模型忽略图结构且依赖外部数据源。需要一种同时兼顾结构和语义的方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段通过在KG内聚类语义相关实体和关系生成候选三元组；第二阶段使用轻量级嵌入过滤后进行LLM语义验证。完全基于内部KG，不依赖外部数据源，针对LLM生成图中最常见的隐式语义。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，OMNIA相比传统嵌入模型显著提升了F1分数。聚类和过滤阶段有效降低了搜索空间和验证成本，同时保持了高质量补全效果。

Conclusion: OMNIA有效融合了结构推理与语义推理，在知识图谱补全任务中展现出优越的性能和效率平衡，为处理LLM生成的知识图谱提供了一种新的有效方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to represent structured knowledge, yet their automatic construction, especially with Large Language Models (LLMs), often results in incomplete or noisy outputs. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to infer and add missing triples, but most existing methods either rely on structural embeddings that overlook semantics or language models that ignore the graph's structure and depend on external sources. In this work, we present OMNIA, a two-stage approach that bridges structural and semantic reasoning for KGC. It first generates candidate triples by clustering semantically related entities and relations within the KG, then validates them through lightweight embedding filtering followed by LLM-based semantic validation. OMNIA performs on the internal KG, without external sources, and specifically targets implicit semantics that are most frequent in LLM-generated graphs. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that OMNIA significantly improves F1-score compared to traditional embedding-based models. These results highlight OMNIA's effectiveness and efficiency, as its clustering and filtering stages reduce both search space and validation cost while maintaining high-quality completion.

</details>


### [59] [Numerical benchmark for damage identification in Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12069)
*Francesca Marafini,Giacomo Zini,Alberto Barontini,Nuno Mendes,Alice Cicirello,Michele Betti,Gianni Bartoli*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对结构健康监测(SHM)领域数据获取困难的挑战，提出了一套模拟SHM数据集生成框架。该数据集包含加速度和位移测量，整合了环境运营变化、损伤类型、测量噪声和传感器故障等因素，采用单自由度和欧拉-伯努利梁模型生成，并通过并行计算实现，最终以开源方式发布供研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工程领域特别是结构健康监测(SHM)中，数据驱动策略和混合物理数据方法的验证数据获取面临重大挑战。数据所有权、安全性、可访问性和元数据便捷性等问题阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 采用单自由度(SDOF)系统生成加速度记录，欧拉-伯努利梁模型生成位移测量值。运用并行计算技术，考虑环境与运营变化(EOV)、不同类型损伤、测量噪声及传感器故障等影响因素，生成了动态和静态测量数据。

Result: 生成了包含动态加速度和静态位移测量的综合模拟SHM数据集，涵盖固定-固定钢梁结构的数值基准测试。数据以开源形式发布，包含数据类型、格式和采集方法的详细描述。

Conclusion: 该数据集为SHM领域的研究提供了可访问、高质量的合成数据资源，解决了数据获取困难的问题，促进了验证和验证新数据驱动方法的发展。

Abstract: The availability of a dataset for validation and verification purposes of novel data-driven strategies and/or hybrid physics-data approaches is currently one of the most pressing challenges in the engineering field. Data ownership, security, access and metadata handiness are currently hindering advances across many fields, particularly in Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) applications. This paper presents a simulated SHM dataset, comprised of dynamic and static measurements (i.e., acceleration and displacement), and includes the conceptual framework designed to generate it. The simulated measurements were generated to incorporate the effects of Environmental and Operational Variations (EOVs), different types of damage, measurement noise and sensor faults and malfunctions, in order to account for scenarios that may occur during real acquisitions. A fixed-fixed steel beam structure was chosen as reference for the numerical benchmark. The simulated monitoring was operated under the assumptions of a Single Degree of Freedom (SDOF) for generating acceleration records and of the Euler-Bernoulli beam for the simulated displacement measurements. The generation process involved the use of parallel computation, which is detailed within the provided open-source code. The generated data is also available open-source, thus ensuring reproducibility, repeatability and accessibility for further research. The comprehensive description of data types, formats, and collection methodologies makes this dataset a valuable resource for researchers aiming to develop or refine SHM techniques, fostering advancements in the field through accessible, high-quality synthetic data.

</details>


### [60] [Structure Selection for Fairness-Constrained Differentially Private Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12112)
*Naeim Ghahramanpour,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivCI方法，通过在差分隐私合成数据生成过程中强制执行条件独立性(CI)约束来解决公平性问题。方法采用CI感知的贪婪MST算法，在指数机制下将可行性检查集成到Kruskal算法中。实验表明该方法在满足CI约束的同时，实现了更强的保真度和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私合成器保留数据中的所有依赖关系，包括敏感属性与结果之间的虚假相关性，这在公平性关键场景中会复制不需要的偏差。需要一种原则性方法来强制执行条件独立性约束，以编码领域知识或法律要求，使结果在考虑允许因素后与敏感属性独立。

Method: 提出PrivCI方法，在测量阶段强制执行CI约束。具体采用CI感知的贪婪MST算法，将可行性检查集成到Kruskal算法构建中，使用指数机制选择边以提高精度。

Result: 在标准公平性基准数据集上的实验表明，PrivCI在满足指定CI约束的同时，实现了比基线方法更强的保真度和预测精度。

Conclusion: PrivCI通过在差分隐私合成数据生成过程中有效集成条件独立性约束，成功解决了公平性与隐私的权衡问题，为公平性关键应用提供了一种原则性的解决方案。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) enables safe data release, with synthetic data generation emerging as a common approach in recent years. Yet standard synthesizers preserve all dependencies in the data, including spurious correlations between sensitive attributes and outcomes. In fairness-critical settings, this reproduces unwanted bias. A principled remedy is to enforce conditional independence (CI) constraints, which encode domain knowledge or legal requirements that outcomes be independent of sensitive attributes once admissible factors are accounted for. DP synthesis typically proceeds in two phases: (i) a measure- ment step that privatizes selected marginals, often structured via minimum spanning trees (MSTs), and (ii) a reconstruction step that fits a probabilistic model consistent with the noisy marginals. We propose PrivCI, which enforces CI during the measurement step via a CI-aware greedy MST algorithm that integrates feasibility checks into Kruskal's construction under the exponential mechanism, improving accuracy over competing methods. Experiments on standard fairness benchmarks show that PrivCI achieves stronger fidelity and predictive accuracy than prior baselines while satisfying the specified CI constraints.

</details>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [Context Before Code: An Experience Report on Vibe Coding in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11073)
*Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Aidul Islam,Md Mahade Hasan,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一篇关于在生产环境中使用对话式代码生成工具（vibe coding）的体验报告。报告指出 AI 辅助编码可加速开发但生成的代码缺乏对安全隔离、访问控制等关键架构约束的显式定义，需要架构师进行额外设计验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前对话式代码生成工具在生产环境中应用经验报告不足的问题，探讨在生产约束下进行"氛围编程"的实际效果和局限性。

Method: 体验报告方法论，通过构建两个实际生产系统进行实证研究：（1）多项目智能体学习平台（支持结构化内存、后台处理和项目级隔离）（2）学术检索增强生成系统（提供引用支撑的答案、基于角色的访问控制和评估追踪）。采用情境提示和显式架构约束相结合的方法。

Result:  vibe coding 加速了脚手架搭建和系统集成效率。然而，生成的代码在未明确界定的情况下，往往缺乏对隔离规则和基础设施约束的充分表达。工程努力从样板代码实现转变为约束规范的制定与执行审计。识别出"非授权区域"，在这些区域对话式代码生成无法满足生产可靠性要求。

Conclusion: 对话式代码生成（vibe coding）在脚手架搭建和集成阶段具有显著加速效果，但生成的代码往往缺乏对隔离规则和基础设施约束的明确表达。需要将工程重点从样板代码实现转向约束规范的制定与执行审计。多租户、访问控制、内存策略和异步处理等领域需要架构师进行刻意设计。此外，存在"非授权区域"，在这些区域对话式代码生成无法满足生产级可靠性要求。

结论：vibe coding 改变了软件工程的的工作模式，但不能完全替代架构决策和边界情况的处理。

Abstract: Code-generating tools are increasingly used in software development, yet experience reports on conversational "vibe coding" under production constraints remain limited. This paper presents an experience report from a small full-stack team that applied contextual prompting and explicit architectural constraints to build (i) a multi-project agent learning platform designed for sustained, production-oriented use and (ii) an academic retrieval-augmented generation system.
  The agent platform supports multiple isolated projects, each with structured memory and background processing, thereby enforcing project-level isolation. The RAG system provides citation-grounded answers, role-based access control, and evaluation tracking.
  Across both systems, vibe coding accelerated scaffolding and integration. However, the generated code often under-specified isolation rules and infrastructure constraints when these were not explicitly defined. Consequently, aspects such as multi-tenancy, access control, memory policies, and asynchronous processing required deliberate architectural design and verification. We observe a shift in engineering effort from boilerplate implementation toward constraint specification and enforcement auditing. We also identify recurring architectural "non-delegation zones" where conversational code generation remains insufficient for production reliability.

</details>


### [62] [Quality-Driven Agentic Reasoning for LLM-Assisted Software Design: Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT) as a Time-Series Self-QA Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11082)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Questions-of-Thoughts(QoT)框架，通过推理时scaffolding将用户目标转化为有序工程步骤和分步自我质询，以验证约束、减少遗漏错误。在API设计、数据通信、文件系统三个后端工程领域评估，采用ISO/IEC启发的质量 rubric。发现质量提升与模型规模正相关，大模型和复杂领域收益更显著。发布开源artifact支持可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助软件开发存在实际部署障碍：实现不完整、模块化薄弱、安全实践不一致。现有方法缺乏在推理时对工程约束的系统性验证机制。

Method: QoT将用户目标分解为：(i)有序工程步骤序列；(ii)分步自我质询验证约束、减少遗漏错误；维护轻量级推理记录以稳定后续设计决策。评估覆盖API设计、数据通信、文件系统三个后端工程领域，采用ISO/IEC启发的质量评分标准（可扩展性、完整性、模块化、安全性）。

Result: QoT带来容量依赖性改进：更大模型和更复杂领域获得一致的质量提升；较小模型在紧张上下文和规划预算下可能存在权衡。报告中给出各领域质量分数增益（QoT分数减去NoQoT分数）。

Conclusion: QoT框架证明了在推理时引入结构化思维 scaffolding 可以显著提升LLM生成软件的质量，且收益与模型规模正相关。发布开源 artifact 促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have accelerated AI-assisted software development, yet practical deployment remains constrained by incomplete implementations, weak modularization, and inconsistent security practices. We introduce Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT), a quality-driven inference-time scaffold that turns a user goal into (i) an ordered sequence of engineering steps and (ii) stepwise self-questioning to verify constraints and reduce omission errors, while maintaining a lightweight reasoning record that stabilizes subsequent design decisions.
  We evaluate QoT across three representative backend engineering domains: API Design, Data Communication, and File Systems. Each task requires multi-module decomposition and exposes standard failure modes in LLM-generated systems. To enable data-driven comparison, we score generated artifacts using an ISO/IEC-inspired quality rubric that measures Scalability, Completeness, Modularity, and Security. We report domain-wise gains as the change in total quality score, defined as the QoT score minus the NoQoT score. Results show capacity-dependent improvements: QoT yields consistent quality improvements for larger models and more complex domains, while smaller models may exhibit trade-offs under tight context and planning budgets.
  We release an open artifact with prompts, scoring guidelines, raw generations, and scripts that reproduce the reported tables and figures to support applied AI and data analytics research.

</details>


### [63] [Understanding by Reconstruction: Reversing the Software Development Process for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11103)
*Zhiyuan Zeng,Yichi Zhang,Yong Shan,Kai Hua,Siyuan Fang,Zhaiyu Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Haozhe Wang,Yining Zheng,Ming Ding,Ke Shen,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过重构静态代码仓库背后的agentic轨迹(规划、推理、调试步骤)来为LLM提供更丰富的监督信号，解决了现有预训练数据缺乏中间推理过程的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)在代码生成方面取得成功，但在复杂软件工程所需的深度、长期推理方面存在不足。原因是标准预训练数据仅包含静态软件仓库的最终状态，抽象掉了中间的规划、调试和迭代 refinement过程，缺乏对agentic轨迹的监督信号。

Method: 提出"理解即重构"(understanding via reconstruction)范式，通过多智能体模拟合成轨迹，并基于源代码仓库的结构现实(如依赖图和文件层次结构)确保保真度。采用基于搜索的优化技术迭代改进Chain-of-Thought推理，以最大化对真实代码的似然。

Result: 在重构后的轨迹上进行持续预训练，显著提升了Llama-3-8B在多个基准测试上的性能，包括长上下文理解、编码能力和agentic能力。

Conclusion: 通过理解即重构范式，利用多智能体模拟和搜索优化生成高质量推理轨迹，持续预训练可显著提升LLM在软件工程任务上的长上下文理解、编码和agentic能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in code generation, they often struggle with the deep, long-horizon reasoning required for complex software engineering. We attribute this limitation to the nature of standard pre-training data: static software repositories represent only the terminal state of an intricate intellectual process, abstracting away the intermediate planning, debugging, and iterative refinement. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel paradigm: understanding via reconstruction. We hypothesize that reverse-engineering the latent agentic trajectories -- the planning, reasoning, and debugging steps -- behind static repositories provides a far richer supervision signal than raw code alone. To operationalize this, we introduce a framework that synthesizes these trajectories using a multi-agent simulation. This process is grounded in the structural realities of the source repositories (e.g., dependency graphs and file hierarchies) to ensure fidelity. Furthermore, to guarantee the logical rigor of the synthetic data, we employ a search-based optimization technique that iteratively refines the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to maximize the likelihood of the ground-truth code. Empirical results demonstrate that continuous pre-training on these reconstructed trajectories significantly enhances Llama-3-8B's performance across diverse benchmarks, including long-context understanding, coding proficiency, and agentic capabilities.

</details>


### [64] [Bridging Behavioral Biometrics and Source Code Stylometry: A Survey of Programmer Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11150)
*Marek Horvath,Emilia Pietrikova,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于程序员归因（代码 authorship attribution）的系统性映射研究，从135篇候选论文中筛选出47篇（2012-2025年），分析了源代码分析中的作者识别任务、特征类别、学习方法、数据集来源和评估实践，并提出了特征-技术分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序员归因问题在软件工程、安全和数字取证领域受到广泛关注，但研究方法多样，缺乏统一框架。该研究旨在系统性地梳理和整合该领域现有研究，识别方法论差距并指导未来工作。

Method: 采用系统性映射研究方法，通过结构化筛选流程从135篇候选论文中筛选出47篇符合条件的研究，从作者任务、特征类别、学习建模方法、数据集来源和评估实践等多个维度进行分析。

Result: 研究结果表明：该领域强调查闭世界作者归因和风格度量特征，依赖少量基准数据集；行为信号、作者验证和可复现性方面仍待深入探索。

Conclusion: 研究将现有工作整合为统一框架，提出了风格和行为特征与机器学习技术相结合的分类法，并指出了该领域的主要方法论差距，为未来研究提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Programmer attribution seeks to identify or verify the author of a source code artifact using stylistic, structural, or behavioural characteristics. This problem has been studied across software engineering, security, and digital forensics, resulting in a growing and methodologically diverse set of publications. This paper presents a systematic mapping study of programmer attribution research focused on source code analysis. From an initial set of 135 candidate publications, 47 studies published between 2012 and 2025 were selected through a structured screening process. The included works are analysed along several dimensions, including authorship tasks, feature categories, learning and modelling approaches, dataset sources, and evaluation practices. Based on this analysis, we derive a taxonomy that relates stylistic and behavioural feature types to commonly used machine learning techniques and provide a descriptive overview of publication trends, benchmarks, programming languages. A content-level analysis highlights the main thematic clusters in the field. The results indicate a strong focus on closed-world authorship attribution using stylometric features and a heavy reliance on a small number of benchmark datasets, while behavioural signals, authorship verification, and reproducibility remain less explored. The study consolidates existing research into a unified framework and outlines methodological gaps that can guide future work. This manuscript is currently under review. The present version is a preprint.

</details>


### [65] [ExecVerify: White-Box RL with Verifiable Stepwise Rewards for Code Execution Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11226)
*Lingxiao Tang,He Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Muyang Ye,Zhongxin Liu,Xiaoxue Ren,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExecVerify是一种通过强化学习训练代码大模型进行代码执行推理的方法，通过引入基于执行轨迹的可验证白盒奖励（包括下一语句预测和变量值/类型预测），结合约束程序合成构建多难度级别数据集，使7B模型在代码推理基准上达到与32B模型相当的性能，并在代码生成任务上将pass@1提升最多5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLMs在代码执行推理方面仍存在困难，尤其是较小的模型。当前基于监督微调(SFT)的方法存在以下问题：1) 中间执行步骤无法在SFT期间被显式验证，导致训练目标退化为仅匹配教师解释；2) 训练数据通常未对任务难度进行显式控制。这促使作者寻求一种能够对齐训练目标与每一步执行语义正确性的方法。

Method: 提出ExecVerify方法，包含三个核心组件：1) 基于约束的程序合成构建多难度级别的数据集；2) 引入可验证的白盒奖励（来自执行轨迹），包括下一语句预测和变量值/类型预测；3) 采用两阶段训练 pipeline，首先增强执行推理能力，然后迁移到代码生成任务。强化学习用于奖励关于中间执行步骤和最终输出的正确答案。

Result: 实验表明，使用ExecVerify训练的7B模型在代码推理基准上达到了与32B模型相当的性能，在代码生成任务上比强基线的后训练方法提升了最多5.9%的pass@1。

Conclusion: 通过将可验证的白盒奖励纳入训练过程，ExecVerify成功地将训练目标与每一步执行的语义正确性对齐。两阶段训练策略有效实现了从执行推理到代码生成的能力迁移，为提升代码LLMs的执行推理能力提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Code LLMs still struggle with code execution reasoning, especially in smaller models. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with teacher-generated explanations, primarily in two forms: (1) input-output (I/O) prediction chains and (2) natural-language descriptions of execution traces. However, intermediate execution steps cannot be explicitly verified during SFT, so the training objective can reduce to merely matching teacher explanations. Moreover, training data is typically collected without explicit control over task difficulty. We introduce ExecVerify, which goes beyond text imitation by incorporating verifiable white-box rewards derived from execution traces, including next-statement prediction and variable value/type prediction. Our work first builds a dataset with multiple difficulty levels via constraint-based program synthesis. Then, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) to reward correct answers about both intermediate execution steps and final outputs, aligning the training objective with semantic correctness at each execution step. Finally, we adopt a two-stage training pipeline that first enhances execution reasoning and then transfers to code generation. Experiments demonstrate that a 7B model trained with ExecVerify achieves performance comparable to 32B models on code reasoning benchmarks and improves pass@1 by up to 5.9\% on code generation tasks over strong post-training baselines.

</details>


### [66] [Unveiling Practical Shortcomings of Patch Overfitting Detection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11262)
*David Williams,Ioakim Avraam,Aldeida Aleti,Matias Martinez,Justyna Petke,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对补丁过拟合检测(POD)技术进行综合基准测试，发现随机选择基线在大多数情况下优于现有6种最先进的POD方法，表明当前POD工具实际应用价值有限，呼吁社区开发新方法并采用真实数据基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动程序修复(APR)可减少开发者调试时间，但生成的补丁可能出现过拟合（通过测试但实际不正确）。现有的补丁正确性评估（过拟合检测）技术缺乏在真实场景下的有效性验证。

Method: 构建反映真实场景的数据集（基于APR工具在相同实验条件下生成的补丁），对6种最先进的POD方法（涵盖静态分析、动态测试和基于学习的方法）进行基准测试，并与两种随机采样基线进行比较。

Result: 简单随机选择在71%至96%的情况下优于所有POD工具，具体取决于POD工具的种类。这一结果令人震惊，表明当前POD工具的实际应用价值有限。

Conclusion: 当前POD工具的实际应用价值有限，随机选择基线在71%-96%的情况下优于现有POD工具。研究呼吁社区开发更有效的POD技术，并采用本研究的基准测试方法进行实际评估。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) can reduce the time developers spend debugging, allowing them to focus on other aspects of software development. Automatically generated bug patches are typically validated through software testing. However, this method can lead to patch overfitting, i.e., generating patches that pass the given tests but are still incorrect.
  Patch correctness assessment (also known as overfitting detection) techniques have been proposed to identify patches that overfit. However, prior work often assessed the effectiveness of these techniques in isolation and on datasets that do not reflect the distribution of correct-to-overfitting patches that would be generated by APR tools in typical use; thus, we still do not know their effectiveness in practice.
  This work presents the first comprehensive benchmarking study of several patch overfitting detection (POD) methods in a practical scenario. To this end, we curate datasets that reflect realistic assumptions (i.e., patches produced by tools run under the same experimental conditions). Next, we use these data to benchmark six state-of-the-art POD approaches -- spanning static analysis, dynamic testing, and learning-based approaches -- against two baselines based on random sampling (one from prior work and one proposed herein).
  Our results are striking: Simple random selection outperforms all POD tools for 71% to 96% of cases, depending on the POD tool. This suggests two main takeaways: (1) current POD tools offer limited practical benefit, highlighting the need for novel techniques; (2) any POD tool must be benchmarked on realistic data and against random sampling to prove its practical effectiveness. To this end, we encourage the APR community to continue improving POD techniques and to adopt our proposed methodology for practical benchmarking; we make our data and code available to facilitate such adoption.

</details>


### [67] [Enhancing Requirements Traceability Link Recovery: A Novel Approach with T-SimCSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11800)
*Ye Wang,Wenqing Wang,Kun Hu,Qiao Huang,Liping Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出T-SimCSE方法，利用预训练语言模型SimCSE自动恢复需求追踪链接。通过计算需求与目标工件之间的相似度，并使用特异性指标重新排序，在10个公共数据集上实现了优于现有方法的召回率和平均精度均值(MAP)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求追踪在确保软件质量和响应需求变更方面具有重要作用。然而，现有基于预训练语言模型的自动需求追踪链接恢复方法准确率不足，且需要大量标注数据集进行训练，而目前可用的标注数据集非常有限。

Method: T-SimCSE方法首先使用SimCSE模型计算需求与目标工件之间的相似度，然后引入新的度量指标"特异性"(specificity)对目标工件进行重新排序，最后在需求与排名靠前的K个目标工件之间建立追踪链接。

Result: 在10个公共数据集上的实验结果表明，T-SimCSE在召回率(Recall)和平均精度均值(MAP)两个指标上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: T-SimCSE能够有效解决需求追踪链接恢复中标注数据不足的问题，通过SimCSE的对比学习框架和无监督训练特性，结合特异性度量指标，显著提升了需求追踪链接恢复的准确性。

Abstract: Requirements traceability plays an important role in ensuring software quality and responding to changes in requirements. Requirements trace links (such as the links between requirements and other software artifacts) underpin the modeling and implementation of requirements traceability. With the rapid development of artificial intelligence, more and more pre-trained language models (PLMs) techniques are applied to the automatic recovery of requirements trace links. However, the requirements traceability links recovered by these approaches are not accurate enough, and many approaches require a large labeled dataset for training. Currently, there are very few labeled datasets available. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel requirements traceability link recovery approach called T-SimCSE, which is based on a PLM -- SimCSE. SimCSE has the advantages of not requiring labeled data, having broad applicability, and performing well. T-SimCSE firstly uses the SimCSE model to calculate the similarity between requirements and target artifacts, and employs a new metric (i.e. specificity) to reorder those target artifacts. Finally, the trace links are created between the requirement and the top-K target artifacts. We have evaluated T-SimCSE on ten public datasets by comparing them with other approaches. The results show that T-SimCSE achieves superior performance in terms of recall and Mean Average Precision (MAP).

</details>


### [68] [QUARE: Multi-Agent Negotiation for Balancing Quality Attributes in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11890)
*Haowei Cheng,Milhan Kim,Foutse Khomh,Teeradaj Racharak,Nobukazu Yoshioka,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Hironori Washizaki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QUARE是一个多智能体框架，通过五个质量专业化智能体（安全、效率、绿色、信任、责任）之间的结构化谈判来自动化需求工程。该框架引入辩证谈判协议来解决跨质量冲突，并将结果转换为拓扑验证的KAOS目标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程(RE)对软件成功至关重要，但自动化仍具挑战性，因为需要平衡多个往往相互冲突的质量属性同时保留涉众意图。现有的大型语言模型(LLM)方法主要依赖单一推理或隐式聚合，无法系统地揭示和解决跨质量冲突。

Method: 提出QUARE（质量感知需求工程）多智能体框架，将需求分析表述为五个质量专业化智能体之间的结构化谈判，由专用协调器协调。QUARE引入辩证谈判协议，通过迭代提案、批判和综合来明确暴露跨质量冲突并解决冲突。谈判结果通过拓扑验证转换为结构正确的KAOS目标模型，并通过检索增强生成(RAG)根据行业标准进行验证。

Result: 在五个案例研究上进行评估（来自MARE、iReDev等成熟RE基准测试和工业自动驾驶规范），涵盖安全关键、金融和信息系统领域。结果显示：QUARE达到98.2%的合规覆盖率（比两个基线提高105%），94.9%的语义保留率（比最佳基线提高2.3个百分点），高可验证性（4.96/5.0），生成的需求比现有多智能体RE框架多25-43%。

Conclusion: 有效的RE自动化 less依赖于模型规模，而更多地依赖于原则性的架构分解、明确的交互协议和自动化验证。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is critical to software success, yet automating it remains challenging because multiple, often conflicting quality attributes must be balanced while preserving stakeholder intent. Existing Large-Language-Model (LLM) approaches predominantly rely on monolithic reasoning or implicit aggregation, limiting their ability to systematically surface and resolve cross-quality conflicts. We present QUARE (Quality-Aware Requirements Engineering), a multi-agent framework that formulates requirements analysis as structured negotiation among five quality-specialized agents (Safety, Efficiency, Green, Trustworthiness, and Responsibility), coordinated by a dedicated orchestrator. QUARE introduces a dialectical negotiation protocol that explicitly exposes inter-quality conflicts and resolves them through iterative proposal, critique, and synthesis. Negotiated outcomes are transformed into structurally sound KAOS goal models via topology validation and verified against industry standards through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We evaluate QUARE on five case studies drawn from established RE benchmarks (MARE, iReDev) and an industrial autonomous-driving specification, spanning safety-critical, financial, and information-system domains. Results show that QUARE achieves 98.2% compliance coverage (+105% over both baselines), 94.9% semantic preservation (+2.3 percentage points over the best baseline), and high verifiability (4.96/5.0), while generating 25-43% more requirements than existing multi-agent RE frameworks. These findings suggest that effective RE automation depends less on model scale than on principled architectural decomposition, explicit interaction protocols, and automated verification.

</details>


### [69] [A Collaborative and Pattern-Based Training Approach to Knowledge Acquisition and Decision-Making During the Design of Software Architectures Courses: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11904)
*Wilson Libardo Pantoja Yepez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Julio Ariel Hurtado Alegría*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文描述了哥伦比亚Cauca大学与阿根廷La Plata大学之间的软件架构协作学习项目，旨在应用和评估培训模式，识别可复制的有效实践。项目通过比较学习目标、课程和教学策略，实施选定的培训模式，并基于技术接受模型(TAM)评估其对学生能力培养的效果。最终提出了一套培训模式目录，以帮助模拟行业环境并结构化架构知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软件架构教育中的关键挑战：架构概念的抽象性、先决知识要求、真实项目环境重现困难、团队协作挑战以及资源限制。行业需要软件架构设计技能，但传统教学难以有效培养学生的架构决策能力。

Method: 1. 规划阶段：两校比较学习目标、课程和教学策略；2. 实施阶段：选定培训模式的实施；3. 评估阶段：基于技术接受模型(TAM)评估培训模式的有效性，包括能力达成度、感知有用性和易用性；4. 整合活动：两校团队合并进行全球化开发迭代。

Result: 1. 识别出有效的培训模式可供其他情境复制；2. 建立了培训模式目录，帮助模拟行业环境和结构化架构知识；3. 架构决策能力需通过时间和多个项目经验逐步培养；4. 验证了协作学习在软件架构教育中的价值。

Conclusion: 成功的软件架构教育需要实用、结构化的培训项目。培训模式能够有效模拟行业环境并促进渐进式学习。两校协作学习模式为跨国软件工程教育提供了可借鉴的经验，具有在其他教育情境中推广的价值。

Abstract: This article describes a collaborative learning experience on Software Architecture (SA) between Universidad del Cauca (UNICAUCA) in Colombia and Universidad Nacional de la Plata (UNPL) in Argentina. The goal was to apply and evaluate training patterns, identifying effective practices for replication in other contexts. During the planning phase, both universities compared learning objectives, curricula, and teaching strategies to find common ground for improving student training. Selected training patterns were implemented, and their impact on professors and students was measured. As an integrating activity, a global development experience was carried out in the final part of the course, merging the work teams of the two educational institutions in a development iteration. The evaluation of this experience focused on the competencies achieved through the training patterns, their perceived usefulness, and ease of use based on the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM). The training addressed industry needs for software architecture design skills despite challenges such as the abstract nature of architectures, prerequisite knowledge, difficulty in recreating realistic project environments, team collaboration challenges, and resource limitations. A catalog of training patterns was proposed to provide quality training. These patterns help simulate industry-like environments and structure architectural knowledge for incremental learning. The ability to make architectural decisions is developed over time and through multiple project experiences, emphasizing the need for practical, well-structured training programs.

</details>

### [70] [Comparison of Outlier Detection Algorithms on String Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11049)
*Philip Maus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究字符串数据的异常检测问题，提出了两种算法：基于Levenshtein距离的局部异常因子(LOF)变体算法和基于层次左正则表达式学习器的异常检测算法，并通过实验验证了两种方法在字符串数据异常检测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测是机器学习中的重要研究课题，但现有研究主要聚焦于数值数据的异常检测，对字符串数据的异常检测研究较少。开发鲁棒的字符串数据异常检测算法对于数据清洗和系统日志文件中的异常检测具有重要应用价值。

Method: 1. 提出一种改进的局部异常因子(LOF)算法，使用Levenshtein距离计算数据集密度，并引入考虑层次字符类的加权Levenshtein距离 measure; 2. 提出基于层次左正则表达式学习器的新型异常检测算法，通过推断期望数据的正则表达式来识别异常。

Result: 实验表明两种算法均能有效识别字符串数据中的异常。正则表达式算法在期望值具有与异常值明显不同的结构时表现优异; LOF算法在异常值与期望值的编辑距离足够不同于期望数据内部编辑距离时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 两种算法各有优劣：正则表达式算法适合结构差异明显的场景，LOF算法适合编辑距离差异明显的场景。应根据数据特性选择合适的异常检测方法。

Abstract: Outlier detection is a well-researched and crucial problem in machine learning. However, there is little research on string data outlier detection, as most literature focuses on outlier detection of numerical data. A robust string data outlier detection algorithm could assist with data cleaning or anomaly detection in system log files. In this thesis, we compare two string outlier detection algorithms. Firstly, we introduce a variant of the well-known local outlier factor algorithm, which we tailor to detect outliers on string data using the Levenshtein measure to calculate the density of the dataset. We present a differently weighted Levenshtein measure, which considers hierarchical character classes and can be used to tune the algorithm to a specific string dataset. Secondly, we introduce a new kind of outlier detection algorithm based on the hierarchical left regular expression learner, which infers a regular expression for the expected data. Using various datasets and parameters, we experimentally show that both algorithms can conceptually find outliers in string data. We show that the regular expression-based algorithm is especially good at finding outliers if the expected values have a distinct structure that is sufficiently different from the structure of the outliers.
